1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the affine object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 680. The moduli space has dimension at most 527 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 680 + O(1/n^527). Summing over all modes and applying the affine convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
